    // 以下为旧版原方法
    function ajaxDataValue(re) {
       if(!re)return re;
        re = re.replace(/&/gm,"%26");
        re = re.replace(/\+/gm,"%2B");
        re = re.replace(/\"/gm,"%22");
        return re;
    }
    function showSuccess(text,title="操作成功") {
        $.gritter.add({class_name:'gritter-success', title: title, text: text, time: 2000, sticky: false});
    }
    function showError(text,title="操作失败") {
        // if(showError.caller && showError.caller.name == 'success' && showError.caller.arguments[0].requestId) {
          // 日志量比较大，暂不开启requestId提示
          // title = title + '<span style="font-size:12px;">(RequestId:'+showError.caller.arguments[0].requestId+')</span>'
        // }
        $.gritter.add({class_name:'gritter-error', title: title, text: text, time: 2000, sticky: false});
    }
    var loadMbModalUrls = {};
    function loadMbModal(tag, url, callback) {
        if (loadMbModalUrls[url] || $("#"+tag+"-tag").length == 0) {
            callback && callback(); return true;
        }
        $.ajax({
            url: url, type: "GET",
            success: function (page) {
                loadMbModalUrls[url] = true; $("#"+tag+"-tag").replaceWith(page); callback && callback();
            },
            error: function (error) {
                console.error(error);
            }
        });
    }
    function getQueryString(name) {
      return getUrlVars()[name]
      function getUrlVars() {
        var vars = {}
        var parts = window.location.href.replace(
          /[?&]+([^=&]+)=([^&]*)/gi,
          function (m, key, value) {
            vars[key] = value
          }
        )
        return vars
      }
    }
    function currencySearch(){
        if($("#currencySearchVal").val() == ""){
            $.gritter.add({
                class_name:'gritter-error',
                title: "操作失败",
                text: "请输入订单编号",
                time: 3000,
                sticky: false
            });
            return false;
        }

        $.ajax({
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=order.doQuickOrder",
            type:"post",
            dataType:"json",
            data:{platformOrderId:$("#currencySearchVal").val()},
            beforeSend:function(){$('.loading_large').show(); },
            success:function(r){
                $('.loading_large').fadeOut();
                if(r.success){
                    if(!r.platformOrderId){
                        $("#currency-search").modal({backdrop:'static'});
                        $("#currency-search").find(".currency-search-platformOrderId").html($("#currencySearchVal").val());
                    }else{
                        var url = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/';
                        if(r.tableBase == '4'){
                            url = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=order.historyOrder&platformOrderId='+r.platformOrderId;
                        }else if(r.url){
                            url = r.url;
                        }else{
                            url='';
                                                        if(!url)url = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=order.list&Order_orderStatus=2&searchType=leftQuick&OrderSearch_fuzzySearchKey=Order.platformOrderId&OrderSearch_fuzzySearchValue='+r.platformOrderId;
                        }

                        window.location.href = url;
                    }
                }else{
                  showError(r.message, "操作失败");
                }

            } });

    }

    function PageSettings(){
        var PageSettingsMenu = $("#first-contents").find(".active").text();
        if($("#first-contents").find(".active").attr("data-key") == 'M0010900') PageSettingsMenu = '开发';
        $("#Page-settings").find(".menu-text").html(PageSettingsMenu);
        $("#Page-settings").find("input[name=psMenuKey]").val($("#first-contents").find(".active").attr("data-key"));
        $("#Page-settings").find(".Page-settings-menu").remove();
        var dataDefaultKey = $("#first-contents").find(".active").attr("data-default-key");
        var PageSettingsHtml = '';
        $(".topmenu").find(".active").each(function(){
            if($(this).find("ul a").length > 0){
                PageSettingsHtml+='<div class="form-group Page-settings-menu">';
                if($(this).find("a").eq(0).text())PageSettingsHtml+='<label class="col-sm-3 control-label">'+$(this).find("a").eq(0).text()+':</label>';
                PageSettingsHtml+='<div class="col-sm-9">';
                $(this).find("ul a").each(function(){
                    if($(this).attr("id") == undefined)return true;
                    var menuKey = $(this).attr("id").replace('menu2K','');
                    var checked = dataDefaultKey == menuKey ? 'checked' : '';
                    if(checked == 'checked')$("#Page-settings").find(".Menu_text").html($(this).find(".text").eq(0).text());
                    PageSettingsHtml+='<label class="radio-inline"><input value="'+menuKey+'" type="radio" name="pagesettings" '+checked+'><span>'+$(this).find(".text").eq(0).text()+'</span></label>';
                });
                PageSettingsHtml+='</div>';
                PageSettingsHtml+='</div>';
            }
        });

        $("#alert-Prompt1").after(PageSettingsHtml);
        if($("#Page-settings").find("input[name=pagesettings]:checked").length == 0){
            $("#Page-settings").find("input[name=pagesettings][value=Notopen]").prop("checked",true);
        }

        $("#Page-settings").modal("show");

    }

    var getFailureOrderBol = true;
    function getFailureOrder()
    {
        if(!getFailureOrderBol)return true;
        getFailureOrderBol = false;
        $.ajax({
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=common.getFailureOrder",
            dataType:"json",
            type:"post",
            data:"&adminShopIds=",
            beforeSend:function(){/*$("#failure_T,#failure_J").attr("class","");*/ $("#failure_T,#failure_J").val(""); },
            success:function(r){
                getFailureOrderBol = true;

                if(/^[0-9]*$/.test(r.failure_T)){
                    $("#failure_T").html(r.failure_T);
                } else{
                    $("#failure_T").html('0');
                }
                if(/^[0-9]*$/.test(r.failure_J)){
                    $("#failure_J").html(parseInt(r.failure_J));
                }  else{
                    $("#failure_J").html('0');
                }

            }});

    }

    var forecastAnomalyNumBol = true;
    function forecastAnomalyNum()
    {
        if(!forecastAnomalyNumBol)return true;
        forecastAnomalyNumBol = false;
        $.ajax({
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=order.getForecastAnomalyNum",
            dataType:"json",
            type:"post",
            data:"",
            beforeSend:function(){ $("#forecastAnomalyNum").val(""); },
            success:function(r){
                forecastAnomalyNumBol = true;
                if(r.forecastAnomalyNum!=undefined && /^[0-9]*$/.test(r.forecastAnomalyNum)){
                    $("#forecastAnomalyNum").html(r.forecastAnomalyNum);
                } else{
                    $("#forecastAnomalyNum").html('0');
                }
            }});
    }

    function jumpNotice(){
        var menuKey = $("#first-contents").find(".active a").attr("data-class");
        if(menuKey == undefined)menuKey = '';
        else menuKey = menuKey.substring(1);
        if(menuKey == ''){
            $(".topmenu").find(".open").each(function(){
                if(menuKey == '' && $(this).hasClass("active"))menuKey = $(this).attr("data-menu");
            });
        }
        window.open("https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=notice.list&menuKey="+menuKey);

    }

    function sidebarMinimize(obj){
                if($(obj).hasClass("minimized")){
            $.cookie('sidebar-minimize-cookie', '', { expires: 365 });
        }else{
            $.cookie('sidebar-minimize-cookie', 'sidebar-minimized', { expires: 365 });
        }
        

    }

    function currencySearchSubmit2(obj){
        if($("#Re-selection-platformId").val() == "" ) {
            showError("请选择平台", "操作失败");
            return false;
        }
        if($("#Re-selection-shop").val() == "" ) {
            showError("请选择店铺", "操作失败");
            return false;
        }

        if($("#Re-selection-platformId").val() == "1" && $("#currency-search-datepicker").val()=="") {
            showError("请输入订单日期", "操作失败");
            return false;
        }

        $.ajax({
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=combosku.menuLeftDownLoadOrder",
            type:"post",
            dataType:"json",
            data:{platformOrderId:$("#currencySearchVal").val(),platformId:$("#Re-selection-platformId").val(),shopId:$("#Re-selection-shop").val(),time:$("#currency-search-datepicker").val()},
            beforeSend:function(){$("#currency-search-loading").removeClass("hide");$(obj).prop("disabled",true); },
            success:function(r){
                $("#currency-search-loading").addClass("hide");$(obj).prop("disabled",false);
                if(r.success){
                    $("#currency-search").modal("hide");
                    currencySearch();
                }else{
                    showError(r.message, "操作失败");
                }

            } });
    }

    function currencySearchSubmit(){
        if($("#currency-search-platformOrderId-val").val() == ""){
            showError("请输入订单编号", "操作失败");
            return false;
        }
        $("#currencySearchVal").val($("#currency-search-platformOrderId-val").val());
        $("#currency-search").modal("hide");
        currencySearch();
    }


    function doChangeShopOption(obj,name,type){
        var platformId = $(obj).val();
                var shopHtml = '<option value="">请选择店铺</option>';var shopDat =  jQuery.parseJSON('[]');


        var type  = type ? type : "option";
        $.each(shopDat,function(k,v){
            if(platformId == v.type){
                v.name = v.name.replace(/%22/,"'");
                if(type == "option")shopHtml+='<option value="'+k+'" data-status="'+v.status+'" data-tokenflag="'+v.tokenflag+'">'+v.name+'</option>';
            }
        })
        $("#currency-search-shop-tokenflag,currency-search-loading").addClass("hide");
        $("#Re-selection-datepicker").addClass("dis-none");
        if(platformId == '1'){
            $("#Re-selection-datepicker").removeClass("dis-none");$("#currency-search-datepicker").val("");
        }
        if(name) $("#"+name).html(shopHtml);
    }


    function doCheckNotice(menuKey,targetMenuUrlKey,obj){
                if(menuKey) $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=notice.doCheckNotice",
            dataType: 'json',
            data:{"menuKey":menuKey},
            success: function(r){
                                if(r.success){
                    window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=notice.list&menuKey="+menuKey;
                }else if(targetMenuUrlKey!='' && $("#menu2K"+targetMenuUrlKey).attr("href")!=undefined && $("#menu2K"+targetMenuUrlKey).attr("href")!=""){
                    if(menuKey == 'M0010900' || menuKey == 'M0020100' ){
                        var url = $("#menu2K"+targetMenuUrlKey).attr("href");
                        window.location.href=replaceParamVal(url,'platform',$(obj).attr("data-platform"));
                    }else{
                        window.location.href=$("#menu2K"+targetMenuUrlKey).attr("href");
                    }
                }else if($("#"+menuKey).find("a").eq(0).attr("href") !=undefined && $("#"+menuKey).find("a").eq(0).attr("href")!=""){
                    if(menuKey == 'M0010900' || menuKey == 'M0020100'){
                        var url = $("#"+menuKey).find("a").eq(0).attr("href");
                        window.location.href=replaceParamVal(url,'platform',$(obj).attr("data-platform"));
                    }else{
                        window.location.href=$("#"+menuKey).find("a").eq(0).attr("href");
                    }
                }
            }});
        tucaoqqchange();
    }

    function changeVersionMenu(isNewMenu){
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.changeVersionMenu",
            dataType: 'json',
            data:{"isNewMenu":isNewMenu},

            success: function(r){$('.loading_large').hide();
                if(r.success){
                    window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/";
                }else{
                  showError(r.message, "操作失败");
                }

            }});

    }

    function doUpdatePassword(ctrl)
    {
        var $form = $("form[name=navigation-formEmployee]");
        $('.loading_large').show();
        $('.has-error').removeClass('has-error').find('#pErrorMessage').remove();
        var $this = $(ctrl);
        $this.prop("disabled", true);
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=employee.doUpdateMyEmployee&id=442183&fromForm=2",
            data: $form.serialize() ,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                $('.loading_large').fadeOut();
                $this.prop("disabled", false);
                if(r.success) {
                    showSuccess("密码更新成功", "操作成功");
                    $("#changePS form")[0].reset();
                    $("#changePS").modal("hide");
                } else {
                    if(r.field)
                    {
                        var obj = $('input[name="' + r.field + '"]');
                        if($(obj).parent().attr('class') == 'input-group')
                        {
                            $('input[name="' + r.field + '"]').parent().parent().addClass('has-error').append('<p id="pErrorMessage" class="help-block">' + r.message + '</p>');
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            $('input[name="' + r.field + '"]').parent().addClass('has-error').append('<p id="pErrorMessage" class="help-block">' + r.message + '</p>');
                        }
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        showError(r.message, "更新失败");
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }



    function changeMenuStatus()
    {
        var status = $.cookie('CookieLeftMenuStatus');
        if(status == null || status == 'undefined' || status == '')
            $.cookie('CookieLeftMenuStatus', 'sidebar-minimized',  {expires: 365});
        else
            $.cookie('CookieLeftMenuStatus', '');
    }

    function menu2KM0010302()
    {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getAbnormalListLabelCount",
            data: "&noFindRole=1&lableIds=" ,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success){
                    var json = eval(r.menu);
                    $.each( json, function(i, n){
                        $("#stepDiv_3").find("a").each(function(){
                            if($(this).attr("data-custom") =="2" && $(this).attr("data-label") == i){
                                $(this).find(".label-info").html(n);
                            }else if($(this).attr("data-custom") =="1" && i == "last"){
                                $(this).find("#wNumber").html(n);
                                if($(this).attr('data-labelids')!='all'){
                                    $(this).attr("data-label",r.lastTypeData);
                                }
                            }else if($(this).attr("data-custom") =="1" && $(this).attr("data-labelId") == i){
                                $(this).find(".label-info").html(n);
                            }

                        });

                    });
                }

            }});
    }

    /* 自动设置左侧菜单待审核消息数目 */
    function setMessageCount()
    {

            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getMessageCount",
            data: "&noFindRole=1" ,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.login == '1') {
                    $("#messageCount_a,#importSystemCount_a").addClass("hide");
                    $('#messageCount').html(r.messageCount);
                    $('#importSystemCount').html(r.importSystemCount);
                    if(r.messageCount>0)$("#messageCount_a").removeClass("hide");
                    if(r.importSystemCount>0)$("#importSystemCount_a").removeClass("hide");
                }
            }
        });
    }

    // 统一菜单计数函数 - 收集所有key并批量请求
    function autoLoadMenuCounts() {
        console.log("开始执行计数");
        // 收集所有需要计数的key
        var countKeys = [];
        var keyElements = {};
        
        // 查找所有带有data-count-key属性的计数元素
        $('[data-count-key]').each(function() {
            var $element = $(this);
            var countKey = $element.attr('data-count-key');
            
            // 如果元素内容为空，则收集key
            if ($element.html().trim() === '') {
                if (countKeys.indexOf(countKey) === -1) {
                    countKeys.push(countKey);
                }
                // 记录每个key对应的元素
                if (!keyElements[countKey]) {
                    keyElements[countKey] = [];
                }
                keyElements[countKey].push($element);
            }
        });
        console.log("收集到的key:", countKeys);
        // 如果有需要计数的key，则批量请求
        if (countKeys.length > 0) {
            console.log('需要计数的keys:', countKeys);
            batchGetMenuCounts(countKeys, keyElements);
        }
    }

    // 通用的case计数函数
    function getCaseCount(menuKey) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getCaseCount",
            data: "&menuKey=" + menuKey,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r) {
                if (r.success) {
                    $("#case_" + menuKey).html(r.count);
                }
            }
        });
    }
    
    // 多字段计数函数 - 一个接口返回多个字段，分别更新到不同元素
    // 批量获取菜单计数 - 统一接口
    function batchGetMenuCounts(countKeys, keyElements) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.batchGetMenuCounts",
            data: "&countKeys=" + countKeys.join(','),
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r) {
                if (r.success) {
                    // 遍历返回的数据，更新对应的元素
                    $.each(r.data, function(key, count) {
                        if (keyElements[key]) {
                            keyElements[key].forEach(function($element) {
                                $element.html(count);
                            });
                        }
                    });
                    console.log('批量计数更新完成');
                } else {
                    console.warn('批量获取计数失败:', r.message);
                }
            },
            error: function() {
                console.error('批量获取计数请求失败');
            }
        });
    }
    
    // 清除菜单计数缓存 - 根据key清除
    // 清除菜单计数缓存 - 根据key清除
    function clearMenuCountCache(menuKey) {
        // 根据menuKey直接构造要清除的countKeys
        var countKeys = [];
        countKeys.push('count_'+menuKey);
        
        if (countKeys.length > 0) {
            console.log('准备清除缓存，menuKey:', menuKey, 'countKeys:', countKeys);
            
            // 阻止默认的链接跳转行为
            var $link = $('a[data-mkey="' + menuKey + '"]');
            var originalHref = $link.attr('href');
            
            // 临时禁用链接
            $link.attr('href', 'javascript:void(0);');
            
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.clearMenuCountCache",
                data: "&countKeys=" + countKeys.join(','),
                dataType: 'json',
                timeout: 2000, // 1秒超时
                success: function(r) {
                    if (r.success) {
                        console.log('菜单计数缓存已清除:', countKeys);
                    } else {
                        console.warn('清除菜单计数缓存失败:', r.message);
                    }
                    // 请求完成后恢复链接并跳转
                    $link.attr('href', originalHref);
                    window.location.href = originalHref;
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error('清除菜单计数缓存请求失败:', status, error);
                    // 请求失败也恢复链接并跳转
                    $link.attr('href', originalHref);
                    window.location.href = originalHref;
                }
            });
            
            // 阻止事件冒泡
            return false;
        } else {
            console.log('该菜单没有计数元素，无需清除缓存:', menuKey);
            // 如果没有需要清除的缓存，允许正常跳转
            return true;
        }
    }

        e.stopPropagation()
        $("#mb-user,#mb-products,#mb-language").removeClass("active")
        $(this).addClass("active").siblings().removeClass("active")
        if ($(this).find(".sub").length) {
            var outerWidth = $(this).offset().left + $(this).find(".sub").width() - $(window).width()
            if (outerWidth > 0) {
                $(this).find(".sub").css("marginLeft", -outerWidth + 'px')
            }
        }
    })
    $("#mb-user,#mb-products,#mb-language").bind("click", function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation()
        $("#mb-user,#mb-products,#mb-language").removeClass("active")
        $("#mb-nav>li").removeClass("active")
        $(this).addClass("active")
    })

    function findadvice(){
        var menuKey = $("#first-contents").find(".active a").attr("data-class");
        if(menuKey == undefined)
            menuKey = '';
        else
            menuKey = menuKey.substring(1);
        $.ajax({
            type:"POST",
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=advice.findadvice",
            data:{menuKey:menuKey,type:1},
            async: false,
            dataType:'json',
            success:function(r){
                if(r.success){
                    $("#website-notice").html(r.html);
                    $('#website-history').html(r.bightml);
                }
            }
        });
        $("#tucao-panel").addClass("active");
    }

    function currencySkuSearch(){
        if($('#currencySearchSkuVal').val()==''){
            $.gritter.add({
                class_name:'gritter-error',
                title: "操作失败",
                text: "请输入sku",
                time: 2000,
                sticky: false
            });
        }
        $.ajax({
            type:"POST",
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=stock.listfastfindsku",
            data:{sku:$('#currencySearchSkuVal').val()},
            dataType:'json',
            beforeSend:function(){$('.loading_large').show();},
            success:function(r){
                $('.loading_large').fadeOut();
                if(r.success){
                    window.location.href=r.url;
                }else{
                    showError(r.message, "查找失败");
                }
            }
        });
    }

    $(document).on('mouseenter', '.platform-menu-unauthorized-card', function(){
        var cardRect = this.getBoundingClientRect();
        var scroller = $(this).closest('.old-tab-con')[0];
        var cardList = $(this).closest('.platform-menu-unauthorized-list')[0];
        var boundaryBottom = scroller ? scroller.getBoundingClientRect().bottom : window.innerHeight;
        var boundaryRight = cardList ? cardList.getBoundingClientRect().right : window.innerWidth;
        this.setAttribute('data-tooltip-align', cardRect.right + 220 > boundaryRight ? 'right' : 'left');
        this.setAttribute('data-tooltip-placement', cardRect.bottom + 150 > boundaryBottom ? 'top' : 'bottom');
    });
    $(document).on('mouseleave', '.platform-menu-unauthorized-card', function(){
        this.removeAttribute('data-tooltip-placement');
        this.removeAttribute('data-tooltip-align');
    });

  function websocketGetCookie(cname) {
      let name = cname + "=";
      let ca = document.cookie.split(";");
      for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
          let c = ca[i].trim();
          if (c.indexOf(name) === 0) {
              return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
          }
      }
  }

  function removeTipsBox(item){
    $(item).parent().parent().remove()
  }

  function clickMessageDetail(item){
    id = $(item).val()
    console.log('开始调用未读')
    updateUnread(id,null)
    console.log('未读调用结束')
    var paramStr = '';
      if(id){
          paramStr = "&id="+id;
      }
      var url = "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.messageCenter"+paramStr;
      //将消息设为已读
      window.location.href=url;
  }

  function messageWebsocketHandleData(data){
      if(data.type == 'MessageCenterEvent'){//未读消息
            if(data.message > 0){
            if($("#unread-data").hasClass("hide"))$("#unread-data").removeClass('hide');
            $("#unread-data").attr('data-num', data.message);
            $("#unread-data").html((data > 99 ? "99+" : data.message));
        }else if(data.message == 0){
            $("#unread-data").addClass('hide');
            $("#unread-data").attr('data-num', 0);
        }
      }
      if(data.type == 'ForceNoticeEvent'){//强提醒
            message = JSON.parse(data.message)
            if(message!=null && message.length > 0){
                message.forEach(function(item,index){
                console.log(item)
                var html = '<div class="tips-box-ria" id="bottomad'+index+'">'    
                html +='<div class="title-st-yt">'
                html +='<b>'+item.title+'</b>'
                html +='<img onclick="removeTipsBox(this)" src="https://global.mabangerp.com/mabangerp/dashboard/images/2022img/svg/close.svg" alt="" style="cursor:pointer"id="customimgcloseFine"></div>'
                html +='<div class="all-content-box">'
                html +='<img  src="https://global.mabangerp.com/mabangerp/dashboard/images/2022img/Group_bord.png" alt="">'
                html +='<div class="content-por">'+item.message+'</div>'
                html +='<button class="btn-one" onclick="clickMessageDetail(this)" value="'+item.id+'">立即查看</button>'
                html +='</div>'
                html +='</div>'
                if($(".tips-box-ria").length == 3){//最多保留三个弹窗
                    $(".tips-box-ria")[0].fadeOut('fast', function() {
                        $(this).remove();
                    });    
                }
                $(html).appendTo('body').delay(5000).fadeOut('fast', function() {
                        $(this).remove();
                });
                $(eval("bottomad"+index)).css({top:50+(100*index)+5+"px"})
                //将推送成功的消息状态设为不提醒
                clearForceNotice(item.id)
                })
            }   
      }
  }

  function clearForceNotice(messageId){

              }
          },

  function getCookie(cname) {
        let name = cname + "=";
        let ca = document.cookie.split(";");
        for (let i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
            let c = ca[i].trim();
            if (c.indexOf(name) === 0) {
                return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
            }
        }
    }

    function showNotice(data) {
        const div = document.createElement("div");
        div.setAttribute('class', 'notice')
        div.innerHTML = data;
        const bodyElement = document.querySelector("body");
        bodyElement.appendChild(div);
    }

  function startWs() {
        // 获取cookie
        const SESSIONID = getCookie("PHPSESSID");
        // 发布订阅
        const broadcastChannel = new BroadcastChannel('sharedWebsocket');//创建一个名字是sharedWebsocket BroadcastChannel对象
        sharedWebsocket = new SharedWebsocket(webSocketUrl+'/mas-erp-websocket-service/ws/newMessageCenter?token=au7jmrolout91e5pohvioajjn3', {
            // reconnectAttempts: 5, //开启前端重连机制
            // reconnectInterval: 60000,// 重连间隔时间
            // enableHeartBeat: true, // 是否开启心跳
             heartBeatInterval: 9000, // 心跳发送时间
            onOpen: () => {
                console.log('连接成功')
                sharedWebsocket.send(JSON.stringify(messaegCenterTotalParams));
            },
            onMessage: (data) => {
                // 发布
                broadcastChannel.postMessage(data);
                //showNotice(data);
                messageWebsocketHandleData(JSON.parse(data));
            },
            onClose: (event) => {
                console.log('关闭成功', event.code);
                // clearInterval(setSocketTime);
                // setSocketTime = null
            },
            onError: (error) => {
                console.log('出现错误', error);
            }
        });
        // 订阅
        broadcastChannel.onmessage = function ({data}) {
            messageWebsocketHandleData(data);
            //showNotice(data)
        }
    }

  // 标记已读
  function updateUnread(msgId, hideObj) {
      $.ajax({
          type: "POST",
          url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.updateUnread",
          data: {id:msgId},
          beforeSend:function(){$('.loading_large').show();},
          dataType: 'json',
          success: function(r){
              $('.loading_large').hide();
              if(r.success) {
                  showSuccess(r.message, '处理成功');
                  if(hideObj)hideObj.hide();
              } else {
                  showError(r.message, '处理失败');
              }
          },
          error:function(){
              $('.loading_large').hide();
              showError(r.message, '处理失败');
          }
      });
  }

  // 消息体展示
  function showMessageCenterDiv(){
    $.ajax({
        type: "GET",
        url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.messageCenterList",
        data: '',
        beforeSend:function(){
          // $('.loading_large').show();
        },
        dataType: 'json',
        success: function(r){
            // $('.loading_large').hide();
            if(r.success) {
                // 消息处理
                var html = '<div class="blank-box"><div></div><p>暂无新消息</p></div>';
                if(r.data.data){
                    var list = r.data.data;
                    var tmpHtml = '';
                    $.each(list, function(i, dataV){
                        tmpHtml += '<div class="cot-box" data-id="'+dataV.id+'">';
                        if (dataV.sub_type == 9) {
                            tmpHtml += '<p class="mouse-style message-detail" style="margin: 15px 0 5px 0;font-size: 14px;font-weight:400;">'+dataV.title+'</p>';
                        } else {
                            tmpHtml += '<p class="mouse-style message-detail" style="margin: 15px 0 5px 0;font-size: 14px;font-weight:400;">'+((dataV.applicant_oper && dataV.applicant_oper.name) ? dataV.applicant_oper.name : '')+'的'+(dataV.title ? dataV.title : '')+'</p>';
                        }
                        tmpHtml += '<div class="bottom-box" style="padding-bottom: 15px;">';
                        tmpHtml += (dataV.sub_type == 1 ? '<span class="orange-span">' : '<span class="blue-span">');
                        tmpHtml += (dataV.sub_type_name ? dataV.sub_type_name : '')+'</span>';
                        tmpHtml += '<span class="time">'+dataV.create_time+'</span>';
                        if(dataV.is_read == 2)tmpHtml += '<span class="marker message-center-is-read mouse-style" style="font-size: 12px;" data-id="'+dataV.id+'">标记已读</span>';
                        tmpHtml += '</div></div>';
                    })
                    html = tmpHtml ? tmpHtml : html;
                }
                $("#message-center-data .content").html(html);
            } else {
                showError(r.message,'获取失败');
            }
        },
        error:function(){
            showError('','获取失败');
        }
    });
    $("#message-center-data").show();
  }

  // 消息体隐藏
  function hideMessageCenterDiv(){
    $("#message-center-data").hide();
  }
            
</script>

<script>
// 子菜单 hover 展开/收起（更丝滑），并兼容触屏点击
var hoverShowDelayMs = 120;
var hoverHideDelayMs = 200;
$('#mb-user')
  .on('mouseenter', '.time-dropdown-submenu', function() {
    var $item = $(this);
    var $submenu = $item.children('.dropdown-menu');
    clearTimeout($item.data('hideTimer'));
    var showTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      $('#mb-user .time-dropdown-submenu .dropdown-menu').not($submenu).hide();
      $submenu.show();
    }, hoverShowDelayMs);
    $item.data('showTimer', showTimer);
  })
  .on('mouseleave', '.time-dropdown-submenu', function() {
    var $item = $(this);
    var $submenu = $item.children('.dropdown-menu');
    clearTimeout($item.data('showTimer'));
    var hideTimer = setTimeout(function() {
      $submenu.hide();
    }, hoverHideDelayMs);
    $item.data('hideTimer', hideTimer);
  })
  // 触屏设备点击展开/收起
  .on('click', '.time-dropdown-submenu > a', function(e) {
    if ('ontouchstart' in document.documentElement) {
      var $submenu = $(this).next('.dropdown-menu');
      $('#mb-user .time-dropdown-submenu .dropdown-menu').not($submenu).hide();
      $submenu.toggle();
      e.preventDefault();
    }
  });
// 主下拉关闭时一并关闭所有子菜单
$('.dropdown').on('hidden.bs.dropdown', function() {
  $('#mb-user .time-dropdown-submenu .dropdown-menu').hide();
});

    function switchNewOld(oldmod, aftervalue) {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.switchNewOldVersion",
            data: {
                oldmod: oldmod,
                aftervalue: aftervalue
            },
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success){
                    clearNewOldVersionCookie(oldmod);
                    location.reload();
                }else{
                    showError(r.message);
                }
            },
            error: function(){
                showError("{lang 新旧版切换失败，请稍后重试}");
            }
        });
    }
    function clearNewOldVersionCookie(oldmod) {
        var cookieNames = [oldmod, oldmod.replace(/\./g, '_')];
        var expireDate = 'Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:00 GMT';
        $.each(cookieNames, function(_, cookieName){
            document.cookie = cookieName + '=; expires=' + expireDate + '; path=/';
            document.cookie = cookieName + '=; expires=' + expireDate;
        });
    }

//获取url中的参数
function getUrlParam(name) {
 var reg = new RegExp("(^|&)" + name + "=([^&]*)(&|$)"); //构造一个含有目标参数的正则表达式对象
 var r = window.location.search.substr(1).match(reg); //匹配目标参数
 if (r != null) return unescape(r[2]); return null; //返回参数值
}
// 添加 或者 修改 url中参数的值
function updateUrlParam(name, val) {
    var thisURL = document.location.href;
    // 如果 url中包含这个参数 则修改
    if (thisURL.indexOf(name+'=') > 0) {
        var v = getUrlParam(name);
        if (v != null) {
            // 是否包含参数
            thisURL = thisURL.replace(name + '=' + v, name + '=' + val);
        } else {
            thisURL = thisURL.replace(name + '=', name + '=' + val);
        }
    } else {
        if (thisURL.indexOf("?") > 0) {
            thisURL = thisURL + "&" + name + "=" + val;
        }
        else {
            thisURL = thisURL + "?" + name + "=" + val;
        }
    }
    location.href = thisURL;
}

            }
        },
    });
}

<script>

    // js统计代码
  
    function waitingRetry(time,obj) {
        var sec = parseInt(time);
        if (sec == 0) {
            $(obj).addClass("defaultcolor").removeAttr("disabled").text("获取验证码");
            sec = parseInt(time);
        } else {
            $(obj).removeClass("defaultcolor").attr("disabled",true).text(sec+"秒后重新获取");
            sec--;
            setTimeout(function() { waitingRetry(sec,obj) }, 1000)
        }
    }

    function closeAmzProRegistration(){
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.closeAmzProRegistration",
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                console.log(r);
            }
        });
    }

    function doAddAccessLog(funId){
        if(funId)$.ajax({
            url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.doAddAccessLog",
            type:"post",
            dataType:"json",
            data:"&funId="+funId,
            success:function(r){ }});
    }

    $('#amzProRegistration').on('click','.sendCodeA',function(){
        var option={};
        if($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=phone]').val()==''){
            showError('请输入手机号');
            return false;
        }
        option.phone=$('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=phone]').val();
        var button=$(this);
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.doSendCodenopic",
            data: option,
            beforeSend:function(){
                button.prop("disabled", true);
            },
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success) {
                    waitingRetry(60,button);
                } else {
                    showError(r.message)
                    button.prop("disabled", false);
                }
            }
        });
    }).on('click','.addAccountA',function(){
        $('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=password]').val($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=phone]').val())
        if($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=contact]').val()==''){
            showError('请输入联系人');
            return false;
        }
        //验证手机号
        if($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=phone]').val()==''){
            showError('请输入手机号');
            return false;
        }
        if($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=pCode]').val()==''){
            showError('请输入手机验证码');
            return false;
        }
        if($('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=password]').val()==''){
            showError('请输入密码');
            return false;
        }
        if($('#province').val()==''){
            showError('请选择省份')
            return false;
        }
        if($('#city').val()==''){
            showError('请选择城市')
            return false;
        }

        var option={};
        var button=$(this);
        option.mobile=$('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=phone]').val();
        option.pCode=$('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=pCode]').val();
        option.password=$('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=password]').val();
        option.contact=$('#amzProRegistration').find('input[name=contact]').val();
        option.province=$('#province').val();
        option.city=$('#city').val();
        option.isAmz=1;
        option.retypepassword=option.password;
                option.phoneareacode='+86';
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.newRegistered",
            data: option,
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success) {
                    $('#amzProRegistration').modal('hide');
                    //展示对应数据
                                        $('#addWechat').find('.namespan').html(r.name);
                    /*$('.addWechatimg').attr('src','https://global.mabangerp.com/image/amazon/'+data.pic);*/
                    $('.addWechatimg').attr('src',r.picurl);
                    $('#addWechat').modal('show');
                } else {
                    showError(r.message)
                    button.prop("disabled", false);
                }
            }
        });
    });

</script>
<script type="text/javascript">
var  double11Flag =1;
    function menu2KM0010301(type)
    {
        /*
        type具体见后端getMabangCount调用
         */
        if(type==undefined || !type)type='';
        //if($("#processingCount").html() == ""){
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getMabangCount",
            dataType: 'json',
            data:{type:type},
            //async:false,
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success){
                    if(type){
                        type=type.split(',');
                    }
                    if(!type || jQuery.inArray('4',type)>-1){
                        $("#abnormalCount").html(r.abnormalCount);
                    }
                    if(!type || jQuery.inArray('2',type)>-1){
                        $("#orspNumber").html(r.processingCount2);
                    }
                    if(!type || jQuery.inArray('1',type)>-1){
                        $("#orneNumber").html(r.processingCount1);
                        $("#allPendingOrderCount").html(r.processingCount1);
                    }
                    if(!type || jQuery.inArray('3',type)>-1){
                        $("#orqsNumber").html(r.canceledCount);
                    }

                    if(!type){
                        $("#ptNumber").html(r.errorOrderIsSyncLogisticsCount);
                        $("#wNumber").html(r.errorOrderIsSyncPlatformCount);
                        $("#vwNumber").html(r.errorOrderVIsSyncPlatformCount);
                        $("#orsofNumber").html(r.deliveredCount);
                        $("#orowNumber").html(r.finishedCount);

                        $("#stepDiv1").html(r.processingCount2);
                        $("#stepDiv2").html(r.processingCount1);
                        $("#orsofNumberAll").html(parseInt(r.deliveredCount)+parseInt(r.finishedCount));
                        $("#ptNumberAll").html(parseInt(r.errorOrderCount));
                        // $("#failure_T").html(r.newSyncOrderAbnormalCount);
                        $("#failure_J").html(r.errorOrderIsSyncLogisticsCount); // 交运异常
                        $("#failure_W").html(r.errorOrderisTrackingCounts); // 上网异常
                                                                    }
                }
            }
        });
        if((!type || jQuery.inArray('5',type)>-1) &&double11Flag==1 ){
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=order.orderMergeList",
                data:{getcount:1},
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function(r){
                    if(r.success){
                        $("#orderMergecount").html(r.orderMergecount);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
        if((!type || jQuery.inArray('6',type)>-1) &&double11Flag==1){
            $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=ordera.doSearchUnpaidOrder",
                data:{getcount:1},
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function(r){
                    if(r.success){
                        $("#unpaidOrder").html(r.unpaidOrdercount);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
        if((!type || jQuery.inArray('7',type)>-1) &&double11Flag==1){
            // @20250825 订单列表添加待揽收，页面有waitPickupOrder字段再去查询
            if($("#waitPickupOrder").length > 0) $.ajax({
                type: "POST",
                url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=ordera.waitPickupOrder",
                data:{getcount:1},
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function(r){
                    if(r.success){
                        $("#waitPickupOrder").html(r.waitPickupOrderCount);
                    }
                }
            });
        }
            }



    function getCloudCount()
    {
        $.ajax({
            type: "POST",
            url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getCloudCount",
            dataType: 'json',
            success: function(r){
                if(r.success){
                    $("#cloudWaitingProcessCount").html(r.cloudWaitingProcessCount);
                    $("#cloudApplyDistributionCount").html(r.cloudApplyDistributionCount);
                    $("#cloudProcessingCount").html(r.cloudProcessingCount);
                    $("#cloudDeliveredCount").html(r.cloudDeliveredCount);
                    $("#cloudCanceledCount").html(r.cloudCanceledCount);
                }
            }});
    }


    var getTargetCompanyBol = true;
function getTargetCompany()
{ 
	 if($("#Beyond-prompt").hasClass("in"))return true;
	 if(!getTargetCompanyBol)return true;
	 getTargetCompanyBol = false;
	 $.ajax({
	         url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getTargetCompany",
	         dataType:"json",
	         type:"post",
	         success:function(r){
	        	 getTargetCompanyBol = true;
		         if(r.message == '1'){
	        		$("#Beyond-prompt").modal("show");  	        		
		         }
		     }});

}

                    closeButton: false,  
                    buttons: {
                        success: {
                            label: "立即购买",
                            className: "btn-primary",

                                $.ajax({
                                    url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=system.doUpgrade",
                                    type:"post",
                                    dataType:"json",
                                    beforeSend:function(){$('.loading_large').show(); },

                                        $('.loading_large').fadeOut();

                                            window.open('https://market.mabangerp.com/yingyong/platform?'+r.url);
                                        }else{
                                            showError('操作失败')
                                        }
                                    }
                                }); 
                                return false;      
                            }
                        },
                        cancel: {
                            label: "取消",
                            className: "btn-default",
                            callback: function () {
                                window.location.href = "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=main.doLogout&type=1&lite=1";
                            }
                        }
                    }
                });
    }

        $("#marketingRuleExpireTips").modal("show");

function closeMarketingRuleExpireTip() {
    $.ajax({
        type: "POST",
        url: "https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=common.closeMarketingRuleExpireTip",
        async: false,
        dataType: 'json',
        data: {confirm: 1},
        beforeSend: function () {
            $('.loading_large').show();
        },
        success: function (r) {
            $('.loading_large').fadeOut();
            $("#marketingRuleExpireTips").modal("hide");
        }
    });
}

   $(".tobottom").click(function(){
		$('html,body').animate({scrollTop: $(document).height() - $(window).height()},500);return false;
	});
 	//到底部隐藏按钮
	$(window).scroll(function(){
	    if($(window).scrollTop() == $(document).height() - $(window).height()){
	        $(".tobottom").fadeOut();
	    }else $(".tobottom").fadeIn();
	});
    function footerGoBack() {
        document.referrer === '' 
        ? window.location.href = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/'
        : window.history.go(-1);
    }
	function getAutoMessageClosed(autoMessageIds){
		 $.ajax({
	           url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.doAutoMessageClosed",
	           dataType:"json",
	           type:"post",
	           data:"ids="+autoMessageIds,
	           success:function(r){}});
   }

	function getAutoMessage(){
      $.ajax({
           url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=approve.getAutoMessage",
           dataType:"json",
           success:function(r){
               if(r.success){
            	   $.gritter.add({
            		title:r.title,
           			text: r.message,
           			class_name: "gritter-light",
           			time: 1000,
           			sticky: true,
           			before_open:function(){
                         $(".gritter-light").remove();
               		},
           			after_open:function(){
           				$(".autoMessageClosed").on("click",function(){
               				if($(this).attr("data-id"))getAutoMessageClosed($(this).attr("data-id"));
               			});

               		},
           			before_close:function(){
               			var autoMessageIds = [];
               			$('.gritter-light').find("a").each(function(){
                   			if($(this).attr('data-id'))
               				autoMessageIds.push($(this).attr('data-id'));

                   		});
               			getAutoMessageClosed(autoMessageIds);
           		    }
           		});
               }

           }})

    }

               var cancelVotoboVipIds = new Array();
               $("input:checkbox[name='cancelVotoboVip']:checked").each(function() {
                   cancelVotoboVipIds.push($(this).val());
               });

               $.ajax({
                   type:"POST",
                   url:"https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=votobo.setSubaccount",
                   async: false,
                   dataType:'json',
                   data:{'addVotoboVipIds':addVotoboVipIds,'cancelVotoboVipIds':cancelVotoboVipIds},
                   beforeSend:function() {$('.loading_large').show(); },
                   success:function(r){
                       $('.loading_large').fadeOut();
                       if(r.success){
                        showSuccess("设置成功")
                       }else{
                        showError("设置失败")
                       }
                   }
               });
           });
        </script>

    $(document).ready(function(){
       // 每5秒刷析页面验证客户信息是否一样
        setInterval(function(){ checkRefreshPage(); }, 5000)
        window.addEventListener('message', function(event){
            var data = event.data; if(data.globalType) { globalResponseIframeRequest(event); }
        }, false);
    })

    /**
     * #注：在main/frame.php页面下也有相同的监听方式，此处主要解决其他方式引用前端页面，例如loadIframeModal, 其中内容跳转当前域地址
     * 子页面请求跳转父页面， 例如：跳转到订单列表
     * window.parent.postMessage({globalType: 'forward', mod: 'order.list', queryParams:{Order_orderStatus: 2});
     * @param  {[type]} data [description]
     * @return {[type]}      [description]
     */
    function globalResponseIframeRequest(event) {
        var data = event.data;
        if(/\.mabangerp\.com/.test(event.origin) && data.globalType) {// 马帮的域名可调用
            switch(data.globalType) {
                case 'forward': //{globalType: 'forward', mod: 'order.list', queryParams:{Order_orderStatus: 2}
                    var queryParmas = [];
                    if(data.queryParams && data.queryParams !== null) {
                        if(typeof data.queryParams === 'object') {
                            for(var field in data.queryParams) {
                                queryParmas.push(field + '=' + encodeURIComponent(data.queryParams[field]));
                            }
                        } else if(typeof data.queryParams === 'string') {
                            queryParmas.push(data.queryParams[0] == '&' ? data.queryParams.substring(1, data.queryParams.length) : data.queryParams);
                        }
                    }
                    let url = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=' + data.mod + (queryParmas.length > 0 ? '&' + queryParmas.join('&') : '');
                    data.mbTarget ? window.open(url) : window.location.href = url;
                    break;
                case 'url':
                    data.mbTarget ? window.open(data.url) : window.location.href = data.url;
                    break;
            }
        }
    }

    function checkRefreshPage(){
        var nowId = '442183';
        var cacheUniqueId = null;
        try{
            if(typeof atob === 'function') {
                cacheUniqueId = JSON.parse(window.atob(decodeURIComponent(getCookie('MABANG_ERP_PRO_UNIQUE_ID'))));
            }
        } catch(e) {}
        // console.log(cacheUniqueId, nowId)
        if(cacheUniqueId && cacheUniqueId.employeeId != nowId) {
            // window.location.reload();
        }
    }
    // 统计模态框使用频率

        },3000);
    })

    // 复制文本到剪贴板
    function copyToClipboardMB(text) {
        if (navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText) {
            navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(function() {
                window.showSuccess("已复制到剪贴板");
            }).catch(function() {
                fallbackCopyMB(text);
            });
        } else {
            fallbackCopyMB(text);
        }
    }

    // 兼容旧版浏览器的复制方法
    function fallbackCopyMB(text) {
        var textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
        textarea.value = text;
        textarea.style.position = 'fixed';
        textarea.style.left = '-9999px';
        document.body.appendChild(textarea);
        textarea.select();
        try {
            document.execCommand('copy');
            window.showSuccess("已复制到剪贴板");
        } catch (err) {
            window.showError("复制失败，请手动复制");
        }
        document.body.removeChild(textarea);
    }
    </script>

/**
 * 子页面请求跳转父页面， 例如：跳转到订单列表
 * window.parent.postMessage({type: 'forward', mod: 'order.list', queryParams:{Order_orderStatus: 2});
 * @param  {[type]} data [description]
 * @return {[type]}      [description]
 */
function responseIframeRequest(event) {
    var data = event.data;
    if(/\.mabangerp\.com/.test(event.origin) && data.type) {// 马帮的域名可调用
        switch(data.type) {
            case 'forward': //{type: 'forward', mod: 'order.list', queryParams:{Order_orderStatus: 2}
                var queryParmas = [];
                if(data.queryParams && data.queryParams !== null) {
                    if(typeof data.queryParams === 'object') {
                        for(var field in data.queryParams) {
                            queryParmas.push(field + '=' + encodeURIComponent(data.queryParams[field]));
                        }
                    } else if(typeof data.queryParams === 'string') {
                        queryParmas.push(data.queryParams[0] == '&' ? data.queryParams.substring(1, data.queryParams.length) : data.queryParams);
                    }
                }
                let url = 'https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=' + data.mod + (queryParmas.length > 0 ? '&' + queryParmas.join('&') : '');
                data.mbTarget ? window.open(url) : window.location.href = url;
                break;
            case 'url':
                data.mbTarget ? window.open(data.url) : window.location.href = data.url;
                break;
            case 'adjustIframeHeight':
                if (data.height !== undefined && data.height !== null) {
                    var iframeId = data.iframeId || 'iframeContent';
                    const iframeObj = document.getElementById(iframeId);
                    if (iframeObj) {
                        iframeObj.style.height = data.height;
                    }
                }
                break;
        }
    }
}
const receiveMessage = function (event) {
    const data = event.data
    console.log("message");
    if(data.type=='process'){
        console.log(event.data);
        process(event);
        return 0;
    }
    //跳转到组合sku页面
    if(data.source==1){
        window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=combosku.list"
    } else if (data.from && data.from == 'erp4.0') {
        if (data.name == 'tb-pdd' && data.type == 'location') {
            //console.log('跳转')
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=purchases.list&orderId="+(data.groupId?data.groupId:'');
        }
        if (data.name == 'online-match' && data.type == 'location') {
            //console.log('跳转')
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=stock.matchingProductNew";
        }

        if (data.name == 'product-inOutOrder' && data.type == 'location') {
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=product.inOutOrder&orderSnType="+(data.orderSnType?data.orderSnType:'')+"&orderSn="+(data.orderSn?data.orderSn:'');
        }

        if (data.name == 'product-transferOrderManage' && data.type == 'location') {
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=product.transferOrderManage&orderSnType="+(data.orderSnType?data.orderSnType:'')+"&orderSn="+(data.orderSn?data.orderSn:'');
        }
        if(data.name == 'temu-delivery-list' && data.type == 'location'){
            window.open("https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=shippbatch.list&platform=temu&version=1&deliverySn="+data.deliverySn,"_blank");
            //window.href="//&platform=temu&version=1&deliverySn="+data.deliverySn;
        }
        if (data.name == 'product-preparesGoodsOrder' && data.type == 'location') {
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=product.preparesGoodsOrder";
        }
        if (data.name == 'product-transitTransportationOrder' && data.type == 'location') {
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=product.transitTransportationOrder";
        }
        //海外仓出库入流水
        if (data.name == 'product-inventoryRecords' && data.type == 'location') {
            window.location.href="https://www.mabangerp.com/index.php?mod=product.inventoryRecords";
        }
    } else if(data.type) {
        responseIframeRequest(event);
    }
    //data是子页面发送的数据，没有类型限制
}
window.addEventListener('message', receiveMessage, false)

var process_timer,process_dian,process_bar_value=0,process_text;
function process(e){
    if (e.data.action=='show') {//显示进度条
        $("#progress-bar").css({width: process_bar_value + "%"}); //设置进度条进度
        $("#progress-text").html(process_text+process_dian);
        $("#progress-container").removeClass("hide"); //开启进度条窗口

        // $("#progress-bar").css({ width : "20%"}); //设置进度条进度
        var timerI=0;
        process_timer=setInterval(function()   //开启循环：每秒出现一次提示框
        {
            timerI++;
            process_dian='';
            for(var tempI=1;tempI<=timerI%6;tempI++){
                process_dian+="."
            }
            $("#progress-bar").css({width: process_bar_value + "%"}); //设置进度条进度
            $("#progress-text").html(process_text+process_dian);
        },e.data.timeLong);
    }else  if (e.data.action=='hide') {//隐藏进度条
        process_bar_value=0;process_text='';
        clearInterval(process_timer);
        $("#progress-container").addClass("hide"); //关闭进度条窗口
    }else if(e.data.action=='changeValue'){
        process_bar_value=e.data.bar_value;
        process_text=e.data.bar_text;
    }else if(e.data.action=='showError'){

    }
}
